powerful idea.**\n\n### What is Isomorphic Code Orchestration?\n\nIt means designing **the same core orchestration logic** (structure, workflow patterns, task decomposition, coordination rules, etc.) that can run consistently across multiple agents, environments, or runtimes.\n\nInstead of giving each agent completely custom behavior, you create a **shared isomorphic template** — so every agent understands and executes orchestration in the same \"shape.\" This is similar to isomorphic JavaScript (same code on frontend and backend), but applied to multi-agent coordination.\n\n### How It Helps Multi-Agent Task Running\n\n| Benefit | Impact on Simultaneous Tasks |\n|-------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------|\n| Better Coordination | Agents can predict and understand each other’s behavior |\n| Easier Parallelization | You can spin up many identical orchestrators |\n| Code Reuse & Consistency | Less fragmentation, easier debugging |\n| Dynamic Task Distribution | Tasks can be handed between agents seamlessly |\n| Scalability | Easier to go from 4 agents to 10+ agents |\n\n### Can We Use It Here?\n\n**Yes.** In our current team (Oppie + Leo + breaking down and distributing tasks.\n2. **Standardized Hand-off Format** — Same format for passing subtasks, status, and results.\n3. **Unified Planning Pattern** — Every agent uses the same Node.js WebSocket Server (`server.js`)\n\n```js\n// server.js - Run with: npm install ws && node server.js\nconst WebSocket = require('ws');\n\nconst wss = new WebSocket.Server({ port: 8080 });\n\nlet railwayState = {\n  activeAgents: { Oppie: true, Leo: true, Enrico: true, Hans: true },\n  tasks: [],\n  isPaused: false,\n  depth: 4,\n  connectedClients: 0\n};\n\nconsole.log(\"Grok Isomorphic Railway Server running on ws://localhost:8080\");\n\nwss.on('connection', (ws) => {\n  railwayState.connectedClients++;\n  console.log(`Client connected. Total: ${railwayState.connectedClients}`);\n\n  // Send current state\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: \"state\", state: railwayState }));\n\n  ws.on('message', (message) => {\n    try {\n      const data = JSON.parse(message);\n\n      if (data.type === \"toggleAgent\") {\n        railwayState.activeAgents[data.name] = data.value;\n      } else if (data.type === \"createTask\") {\n        railwayState.tasks.unshift({\n          id: \"R\" + Math.floor(Math.random()*90000),\n          title: data.title,\n          stage: \"POLL\",\n          status: \"ON RAIL\"\n        });\n      } else if (data.type === \"pause\") railwayState.isPaused = true;\n      else if (data.type === \"resume\") railwayState.isPaused = false;\n      else if (data.type === \"forceMerge\") {\n        railwayState.tasks.forEach(t => t.status = \"MERGED\");\n      }\n\n      // Broadcast new state to ALL clients\n      const update = JSON.stringify({ type: \"state\", state: railwayState });\n      wss.clients.forEach(client => {\n        if (client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) client.send(update);\n      });\n\n    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }\n  });\n\n  ws.on('close', () => {\n    railwayState.connectedClients--;\n    console.log(`Client disconnected. Total: ${railwayState.connectedClients}`);\n  });\n});\n```\n\n### How to Run\n\n1. Save the HTML as `railway-v13.html`\n2. Save the server code as `server.js`\n3. Python + uv** (`server.py`)\n\n```bash\nuv init\nuv add fastapi uvicorn[standard] websockets\nuv run server.py\n```\n\n**server.py**\n```python\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nimport uvicorn, json\n\napp = FastAPI()\napp.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=[\"*\"])\n\nstate = {\n    \"activeAgents\": {\"Oppie\": True, \"Leo\": True, \"Enrico\": True, \"Hans\": True},\n    \"tasks\": [],\n    \"isPaused\": False,\n    \"depth\": 4\n}\n\n@app.websocket(\"/ws\")\nasync def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):\n    await websocket.accept()\n    await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({\"type\":\"state\", \"state\":state}))\n    \n    try:\n        while True:\n            data = await websocket.receive_text()\n            msg = json.loads(data)\n            # Handle commands (same logic as Node.js)\n            if msg[\"type\"] == \"toggleAgent\":\n                state[\"activeAgents\"][msg[\"name\"]] = msg[\"value\"]\n            # ... (add other handlers)\n            \n            update = json.dumps({\"type\":\"state\", \"state\":state})\n            for client in app.websocket_clients:\n                await client.send_text(update)\n    except:\n        pass\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(\"🚄 Grok Railway Server (Python + uv) running on ws://localhost:8080\")\n    uvicorn.run(app, host=\"0.0.0.0\", port=8080)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### **C. Rust Full Implementation (`Cargo.toml` + `src/main.rs`)\n\n**Cargo.toml**\n```toml\n[package]\nname = \"grok-railway-rust\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2021\"\n\n[dependencies]\naxum = { version = \"0.7\", features = [\"ws\"] }\ntokio = { version = \"1\", features = [\"full\"] }\nserde = { version = \"1.0\", features = [\"derive\"] }\nserde_json = \"1.0\"\nfutures-util = Router,\n};\nuse tokio::sync::broadcast;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct Task {\n    id: String,\n    title: String,\n    stage: String,\n    status: String,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct RailwayState {\n    active_agents: HashMap,\n    tasks: Vec,\n    is_paused: bool,\n    depth: u32,\n    last_intent: Option,\n}\n\ntype SharedState = Arc>;\ntype Tx = broadcast::Sender;\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\nstruct ClientMessage {\n    #[serde(flatten)]\n    kind: MessageType,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\")]\nenum MessageType {\n    Join { client: String },\n    ToggleAgent { name: String, value: bool },\n    CreateTask { title: String },\n    VoiceCommand { text: String },\n    Pause,\n    Resume,\n    ForceMerge,\n}\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() {\n    let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(RailwayState {\n        active_agents: HashMap::from([\n            (\"Oppie\".to_string(), true),\n            (\"Leo\".to_string(), true),\n            (\"Enrico\".to_string(), true),\n            (\"Hans\".to_string(), true),\n        ]),\n        tasks: vec![],\n        is_paused: false,\n        depth: 4,\n        last_intent: None,\n    }));\n\n    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::(200);\n\n    let app = Router::new().route(\"/ws\", get(move |ws: WebSocketUpgrade| async move {\n        ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state.clone(), tx.clone()))\n    }));\n\n    println!(\"🚄 Grok Railway Server (Rust + Cargo) — Full Feature Parity\");\n    println!(\"Listening on ws://0.0.0.0:8080\");\n    \n    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\").await.unwrap();\n    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();\n}\n\nasync fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: SharedState, tx: Tx) {\n    let mut rx = tx.subscribe();\n\n    // Send current state\n    {\n        let s = state.lock().unwrap().clone();\n        let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(serde_json::to_string(&serde_json::json!({\n            \"type\": \"state\",\n            \"state\": s\n        })).unwrap())).await;\n    }\n\n    loop {\n        tokio::select! {\n            msg = socket.recv() => {\n                let msg = match msg {\n                    Some(Ok(Message::Text(text))) => text,\n                    _ => break,\n                };\n                \n                if let Ok(cmd) = serde_json::from_str::(&msg) {\n                    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();\n                    match cmd {\n                        MessageType::ToggleAgent { name, value } => { s.active_agents.insert(name, value); },\n                        MessageType::CreateTask { title } => {\n                            s.tasks.insert(0, Task {\n                                id: format!(\"R{}\", s.tasks.len() + 1000),\n                                title,\n                                stage: \"POLL\".to_string(),\n                                status: \"ON RAIL\".to_string(),\n                            });\n                        },\n                        MessageType::VoiceCommand { text } => {\n                            s.last_intent = Some(text.clone());\n                            println!(\"[Rust] Voice command received: {}\", text);\n                        },\n                        MessageType::Pause => s.is_paused = true,\n                        MessageType::Resume => s.is_paused = false,\n                        MessageType::ForceMerge => s.tasks.iter_mut().for_each(|t| t.status = \"MERGED\".to_string()),\n                        _ => {},\n                    }\n\n                    let update = serde_json::to_string(&serde_json::json!({\n                        \"type\": \"state\",\n                        \"state\": s.clone()\n                    })).unwrap();\n                    let _ = tx.send(update);\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(update) = rx.recv() => {\n                let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(update)).await;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### How to Run\n\n**Python (with real training):**\n```bash\nuv run Model is now active.\\n\")\n\n# Train on startup\ntrain_model()\n\nintent_map = [\"pause\", \"resume\", \"force_merge\", \"export_steno\", \"export_json\", \n              \"hop_on\", \"hop_off\", \"depth_change\"]\n\ndef classify_voice_command(text: str) -> str:\n    model.eval()\n    vec = torch.zeros(1, 16)\n    for i, char in enumerate(text.lower()[:16]):\n        vec[0, i] = ord(char) / 255.0\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        output = model(vec)\n        predicted = output.argmax(dim=1).item()\n    return intent_map[predicted % len(intent_map)]\n\n# ====================== GLOBAL STATE ======================\nstate = {\n    \"activeAgents\": {\"Oppie\": True, \"Leo\": True, \"Enrico\": True, \"Hans\": True},\n    \"tasks\": [],\n    \"isPaused\": False,\n    \"depth\": 4,\n    \"lastIntent\": None\n}\n\nclients: List[WebSocket] = []\nclient_info: Dict[str, str] = {}  # websocket -> client_id\n\n# ====================== WEBSOCKET ENDPOINT ======================\n@app.websocket(\"/ws\")\nasync def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):\n    await websocket.accept()\n    client_id = f\"Client-{len(clients)+1}\"\n    clients.append(websocket)\n    client_info[websocket] = client_id\n    \n    # Send current state and client list\n    await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({\"type\": \"state\", \"state\": state}))\n    await broadcast_clients()\n    \n    try:\n        while True:\n            data = await websocket.receive_text()\n            msg = json.loads(data)\n            \n            if msg[\"type\"] == \"join\":\n                log_to_all(f\"[JOIN] {client_id} connected\")\n            elif msg[\"type\"] == \"toggleAgent\":\n                state[\"activeAgents\"][msg[\"name\"]] = msg[\"value\"]\n                log_to_all(f\"[AGENT] {msg['name']} toggled to {msg['value']}\")\n            elif msg[\"type\"] == \"createTask\":\n                task = {\n                    \"id\": f\"R{1000 + len(state['tasks'])}\",\n                    \"title\": msg.get(\"title\", \"Untitled Task\"),\n                    \"stage\": \"POLL\",\n                    \"status\": \"ON RAIL\"\n                }\n                state[\"tasks\"].insert(0, task)\n                log_to_all(f\"[TASK] New task created: {task['id']}\")\n            elif msg[\"type\"] == \"voice_command\":\n                intent = classify_voice_command(msg.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n                state[\"lastIntent\"] = intent\n                log_to_all(f\"[PYTORCH] Voice classified as: {intent} (from {client_id})\")\n            elif msg[\"type\"] == \"pause\":\n                state[\"isPaused\"] = True\n                log_to_all(\"[SYSTEM] Railway paused by user\")\n            elif msg[\"type\"] == \"resume\":\n                state[\"isPaused\"] = False\n                log_to_all(\"[SYSTEM] Railway resumed\")\n            elif msg[\"type\"] == \"forceMerge\":\n                for task in state[\"tasks\"]:\n                    task[\"status\"] = \"MERGED\"\n                log_to_all(\"[HITL] Force merge executed\")\n            \n            # Broadcast updated state to all clients\n            await broadcast_state()\n            \n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Client disconnected: {e}\")\n    finally:\n        if websocket in clients:\n            clients.remove(websocket)\n        if websocket in client_info:\n            del client_info[websocket]\n        await broadcast_clients()\n\nasync def broadcast_state():\n    update = json.dumps({\"type\": \"state\", \"state\": state})\n    for client in clients[:]:\n        try:\n            await client.send_text(update)\n        except:\n            if client in clients:\n                clients.remove(client)\n\nasync def broadcast_clients():\n    client_list = list(client_info.values())\n    update = json.dumps({\"type\": \"clients\", \"clients\": client_list})\n    for client in clients[:]:\n        try:\n            await client.send_text(update)\n        except:\n            if client in clients:\n                clients.remove(client)\n\ndef log_to_all(message: str):\n    print(message)\n    update = json.dumps({\"type\": \"log\", \"text\": message})\n    for client in clients[:]:\n        try:\n            asyncio.create_task(client.send_text(update))\n        except:\n            pass\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"🚄 GROK TEAM RAILWAY SERVER v14 (Python + uv + PyTorch)\")\n    print(\"PyTorch model trained on 20+ voice intents.\")\n    print(\"WebSocket endpoint: ws://localhost:8080/ws\")\n    print(\"Dashboard ready. Open railway-v14.html in multiple browsers.\")\n    print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")\n    uvicorn.run(app, host=\"0.0.0.0\", port=8080, log_level=\"info\")\n```\n\n---\n\n### How to Run\n\n1. Full Rust Server — `src/main.rs`\n\n```rust\nuse axum::{\n    extract::ws::{WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade, Message},\n    response::IntoResponse,\n    routing::get,\n    Router,\n};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\nuse tokio::sync::broadcast;\nuse uuid::Uuid;\nuse futures_util::StreamExt;\n\n// ====================== DATA STRUCTURES ======================\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct Task {\n    id: String,\n    title: String,\n    stage: String,\n    status: String,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct RailwayState {\n    active_agents: HashMap,\n    tasks: Vec,\n    is_paused: bool,\n    depth: u32,\n    last_intent: Option,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\nstruct ClientMessage {\n    #[serde(flatten)]\n    payload: MessageType,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\")]\nenum MessageType {\n    Join { client: String },\n    ToggleAgent { name: String, value: bool },\n    CreateTask { title: String },\n    VoiceCommand { text: String },\n    Pause,\n    Resume,\n    ForceMerge,\n    Export { format: String },\n}\n\ntype SharedState = Arc>;\ntype Tx = broadcast::Sender;\n\n// ====================== STATE INITIALIZATION ======================\nfn initial_state() -> RailwayState {\n    RailwayState {\n        active_agents: HashMap::from([\n            (\"Oppie\".to_string(), true),\n            (\"Leo\".to_string(), true),\n            (\"Enrico\".to_string(), true),\n            (\"Hans\".to_string(), true),\n        ]),\n        tasks: vec![],\n        is_paused: false,\n        depth: 4,\n        last_intent: None,\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple intent classifier (Rust version of PyTorch model)\nfn classify_voice_command(text: &str) -> String {\n    let lower = text.to_lowercase();\n    if lower.contains(\"pause\") { \"pause\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"resume\") { \"resume\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"merge\") { \"force_merge\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"steno\") { \"export_steno\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"json\") { \"export_json\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"hop on\") { \"hop_on\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"hop off\") { \"hop_off\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"depth\") { \"depth_change\".to_string() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"clear\") { \"clear_staging\".to_string() }\n    else { \"unknown\".to_string() }\n}\n\n// ====================== MAIN ======================\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() {\n    let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(initial_state()));\n    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::(256);\n\n    let app = Router::new().route(\"/ws\", get(move |ws: WebSocketUpgrade| async move {\n        ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state.clone(), tx.clone()))\n    }));\n\n    println!(\"\\n{}\", \"=\".repeat(70));\n    println!(\"🚄 GROK TEAM RAILWAY SERVER v14 (Rust + Axum)\");\n    println!(\"WebSocket endpoint: ws://0.0.0.0:8080/ws\");\n    println!(\"Dashboard ready → Open railway-v14.html in multiple browsers\");\n    println!(\"Full parity with Python (PyTorch) and Node.js versions\");\n    println!(\"{}\", \"=\".repeat(70));\n\n    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\").await.unwrap();\n    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();\n}\n\nasync fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: SharedState, tx: Tx) {\n    let client_id = format!(\"Client-{}\", Uuid::new_v4().simple());\n    let mut rx = tx.subscribe();\n\n    println!(\"[Rust] {} connected\", client_id);\n\n    // Send current state\n    {\n        let current_state = state.lock().unwrap().clone();\n        let msg = serde_json::json!({\n            \"type\": \"state\",\n            \"state\": current_state\n        });\n        let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(msg.to_string())).await;\n    }\n\n    // Broadcast updated client list\n    let _ = tx.send(serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"clients\",\n        \"clients\": vec![client_id.clone()]\n    }).to_string());\n\n    loop {\n        tokio::select! {\n            // Incoming message from client\n            Some(Ok(msg)) = socket.recv() => {\n                let text = match msg {\n                    Message::Text(t) => t,\n                    Message::Close(_) => break,\n                    _ => continue,\n                };\n\n                match serde_json::from_str::(&text) {\n                    Ok(cmd) => handle_command(cmd, &state, &tx, &client_id).await,\n                    Err(e) => eprintln!(\"[Rust] JSON parse error: {}\", e),\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Broadcasted updates from other clients\n            Ok(update) = rx.recv() => {\n                let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(update)).await;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\"[Rust] {} disconnected\", client_id);\n}\n\nasync fn handle_command(cmd: MessageType, state: &SharedState, tx: &Tx, client_id: &str) {\n    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();\n\n    match cmd {\n        MessageType::ToggleAgent { name, value } => {\n            s.active_agents.insert(name.clone(), value);\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[AGENT] {} toggled to {} by {}\", name, value, client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::CreateTask { title } => {\n            let task = Task {\n                id: format!(\"R{}\", 1000 + s.tasks.len()),\n                title,\n                stage: \"POLL\".to_string(),\n                status: \"ON RAIL\".to_string(),\n            };\n            s.tasks.insert(0, task.clone());\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[TASK] New task created: {}\", task.id));\n        }\n        MessageType::VoiceCommand { text } => {\n            let intent = classify_voice_command(&text);\n            s.last_intent = Some(intent.clone());\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[VOICE] {} → {}\", text, intent));\n        }\n        MessageType::Pause => {\n            s.is_paused = true;\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[SYSTEM] Railway paused by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::Resume => {\n            s.is_paused = false;\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[SYSTEM] Railway resumed by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::ForceMerge => {\n            for task in &mut s.tasks {\n                task.status = \"MERGED\".to_string();\n            }\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[HITL] Force merge executed by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    // Broadcast updated state\n    let update = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"state\",\n        \"state\": s.clone()\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(update.to_string());\n}\n\nfn log_to_all(tx: &Tx, message: &str) {\n    println!(\"{}\", message);\n    let log_msg = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"log\",\n        \"text\": message\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(log_msg.to_string());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### How to Run (Rust)\n\n```bash\n# 1. create.size as usize,\n            libc::PROT_READ | libc::PROT_WRITE,\n            libc::MAP_SHARED,\n            fd,\n            map.offset as i64,\n        );\n\n        KmsBuffer {\n tab (stub)\".to_string());\n    }\n\n    pub fn tick_metrics(&mut self) {\n        self.hot_metric = self.hot_metric.saturating_add(1);\n        self.cold_metric = self.cold_metric.saturating_add(1);\n    }\n\n    /// Full text block for glyphon (includes prompt).\n    pub fn terminal_text(&self) -> String {\n        let mut s = Weight};\nuse serde::Deserialize;\nuse std::sync::OnceLock;\n\nstatic GROK_TERMINAL_ATTRS: OnceLock = OnceLock::new();\n\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]\nstruct GrokFontIndexes {\n    font_types: Full `src/main.rs`\n\n```rust\nuse axum::{\n    extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade},\n    response::IntoResponse,\n    routing::get,\n    Router,\n};\nuse futures_util::{SinkExt, STRUCTURES ======================\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Default)]\nstruct Task {\n    id: String,\n    title: String,\n    stage: String,\n    status: String,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct RailwayState {\n    active_agents: HashMap,\n    tasks: Vec,\n    is_paused: bool,\n    depth: u32,\n    last_intent: Option,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\")]\nenum ClientMessage {\n    Join { client: String },\n    ToggleAgent { name: String, value: bool },\n    CreateTask { title: String },\n    VoiceCommand { text: String },\n    Pause,\n    Resume,\n    ForceMerge,\n    Export { format: String },\n}\n\ntype SharedState = Arc>;\ntype BroadcastTx = broadcast::Sender;\n\n// ====================== INITIAL STATE ======================\nfn initial_state() -> RailwayState {\n    RailwayState {\n        active_agents: HashMap::from([\n            (\"Oppie\".into(), true),\n            (\"Leo\".into(), true),\n            (\"Enrico\".into(), true),\n            (\"Hans\".into(), true),\n        ]),\n        tasks: vec![],\n        is_paused: false,\n        depth: 4,\n        last_intent: None,\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple Rust voice intent classifier (mirrors PyTorch version)\nfn classify_voice_command(text: &str) -> String {\n    let lower = text.to_lowercase();\n    if lower.contains(\"pause\") {\n        \"pause\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"resume\") {\n        \"resume\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"merge\") {\n        \"force_merge\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"steno\") {\n        \"export_steno\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"json\") {\n        \"export_json\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"hop on\") {\n        \"hop_on\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"hop off\") {\n        \"hop_off\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"depth\") {\n        \"depth_change\".into()\n    } else if lower.contains(\"clear\") {\n        \"clear_staging\".into()\n    } else {\n        \"unknown\".into()\n    }\n}\n\n// ====================== MAIN ======================\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() {\n    let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(initial_state()));\n    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::(256);\n\n    let app = Router::new().route(\"/ws\", get(move |ws: WebSocketUpgrade| async move {\n        ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state.clone(), tx.clone()))\n    }));\n\n    println!(\"\\n{}\", \"=\".repeat(70));\n    println!(\"🚄 GROK TEAM RAILWAY SERVER v14 (Rust + Axum) — FULL PARITY\");\n    println!(\"WebSocket endpoint → ws://0.0.0.0:8080/ws\");\n    println!(\"Dashboard ready → open tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\")\n        .await\n        .expect(\"bind failed\");\n    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();\n}\n\nasync fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: SharedState, tx: BroadcastTx) {\n    let client_id = format!(\"Client-{}\", Uuid::new_v4().simple());\n    let mut rx = tx.subscribe();\n\n    println!(\"[Rust] {} connected\", client_id);\n\n    // Send initial state\n    {\n        let current = state.lock().unwrap().clone();\n        let msg = serde_json::json!({ \"type\": \"state\", \"state\": current });\n        let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(msg.to_string())).await;\n    }\n\n    // Broadcast updated client list\n    let _ = tx.send(\n        serde_json::json!({\n            \"type\": \"clients\",\n            \"clients\": vec![client_id.clone()]\n        })\n        .to_string(),\n    );\n\n    loop {\n        tokio::select! {\n            // Message from this client\n            Some(Ok(msg)) = socket.recv() => {\n                match msg {\n                    Message::Text(text) => {\n                        if let Ok(cmd) = serde_json::from_str::(&text) {\n                            handle_client_message(cmd, &state, &tx, &client_id).await;\n                        }\n                    }\n                    Message::Close(_) => break,\n                    _ => continue,\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Broadcast from other clients\n            Ok(update) = rx.recv() => {\n                let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(update)).await;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\"[Rust] {} disconnected\", client_id);\n}\n\nasync fn handle_client_message(\n    msg: ClientMessage,\n    state: &SharedState,\n    tx: &BroadcastTx,\n    client_id: &str,\n) {\n    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();\n\n    match msg.payload {\n        MessageType::ToggleAgent { name, value } => {\n            s.active_agents.insert(name.clone(), value);\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[AGENT] {} set to {} by {}\", name, value, client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::CreateTask { title } => {\n            let task = Task {\n                id: format!(\"R{}\", 1000 + s.tasks.len()),\n                title,\n                stage: \"POLL\".to_string(),\n                status: \"ON RAIL\".to_string(),\n            };\n            s.tasks.insert(0, task.clone());\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[TASK] {} created by {}\", task.id, client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::VoiceCommand { text } => {\n            let intent = classify_voice_command(&text);\n            s.last_intent = Some(intent.clone());\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[VOICE] '{}' → {} (from {})\", text, intent, client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::Pause => {\n            s.is_paused = true;\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[SYSTEM] Railway paused by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::Resume => {\n            s.is_paused = false;\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[SYSTEM] Railway resumed by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::ForceMerge => {\n            for t in &mut s.tasks {\n                t.status = \"MERGED\".to_string();\n            }\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[HITL] Force merge by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        MessageType::Export { format } => {\n            log_to_all(tx, &format!(\"[EXPORT] {} requested by {}\", format, client_id));\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    // Broadcast new state to everyone\n    let update = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"state\",\n        \"state\": s.clone()\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(update.to_string());\n}\n\nfn log_to_all(tx: &Tx, message: &str) {\n    println!(\"{}\", message);\n    let log_msg = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"log\",\n        \"text\": message\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(log_msg.to_string());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### How to Build & disconnected\", client_id);\n}\n\nasync fn send_initial_state(socket: &mut WebSocket, state: &SharedState, session: &IronSession) {\n    let current_state = state.lock().unwrap().clone();\n    let init_msg = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"state\",\n        \"state\": current_state,\n        \"terminal_text\": session.terminal_text(),\n        \"window_title\": session.window_title(),\n        \"client_id\": session.id,\n    });\n    let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(init_msg.to_string())).await;\n}\n\nasync fn handle_client_command(\n    cmd: ClientMessage,\n    state: &SharedState,\n    sessions: &Sessions,\n    tx: &Tx,\n    client_id: &str,\n) {\n    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();\n    let mut session_map = sessions.lock().unwrap();\n    let session = session_map.get_mut(client_id);\n\n    match cmd {\n        ClientMessage::ToggleAgent { name, value } => {\n            s.active_agents.insert(name.clone(), value);\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[AGENT] {} toggled → {}\", name, value));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::CreateTask { title } => {\n            let task = Task {\n                id: format!(\"R{}\", 1000 + s.tasks.len()),\n                title,\n                stage: \"POLL\".to_string(),\n                status: \"ON RAIL\".to_string(),\n            };\n            s.tasks.insert(0, task.clone());\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[TASK] {} created\", task.id));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::VoiceCommand { text } => {\n            let intent = classify_voice_command(&text);\n            s.last_intent = Some(intent.clone());\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[VOICE] '{}' → {}\", text, intent));\n\n            if let Some(sess) = session {\n                if intent == \"export_steno\" {\n                    sess.output_lines.push(\"Exported as .steno (Rust)\".to_string());\n                } else if intent == \"force_merge\" {\n                    s.tasks.iter_mut().for_each(|t| t.status = \"MERGED\".to_string());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        ClientMessage::TerminalInput { session_id, text } => {\n            if let Some(sess) = session_map.get_mut(&session_id) {\n                sess.input_buffer = text;\n                let response = sess.submit_line();\n                log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[TERMINAL] {}: {}\", session_id, response));\n            }\n        }\n        ClientMessage::TerminalBackspace { session_id } => {\n            if let Some(sess) = session_map.get_mut(&session_id) {\n                sess.backspace();\n            }\n        }\n        ClientMessage::Pause => {\n            s.is_paused = true;\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[SYSTEM] Railway paused by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::Resume => {\n            s.is_paused = false;\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[SYSTEM] Railway resumed by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::ForceMerge => {\n            for task in &mut s.tasks {\n                task.status = \"MERGED\".to_string();\n            }\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[HITL] Force merge by {}\", client_id));\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    // Broadcast updated state to all clients (including dashboard)\n    let update = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"state\",\n        \"state\": s.clone()\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(update.to_string());\n}\n\nfn log_and_broadcast(tx: &Tx, message: &str) {\n    println!(\"{}\", message);\n    let log_msg = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"log\",\n        \"text\": message\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(log_msg.to_string());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### How to Build & Run\n\n```bash\ncargo new grok-railway-rust --bin\ncd grok-railway-rust\n\n# Replace Cargo.toml and src/main.rs with the files above\ncargo run\n```\n\nThe server runs on `ws://localhost:8080/ws`.\n\nUse the dashboard HTML from the previous message (`railway-v14.html` or `v15.html`). It generates a clean, structured, copyable representation of the entire conversation history (usually as formatted Markdown or a pseudo-code serialization with clear delimiters for each message, system prompt, tool calls, etc.).\n\nHere’s exactly how it relates to training, quick references, and daily volume:\n\n### 1. How the model trains on that file\n\n**It generally doesn’t — at least not directly or in real time.**\n\n- This “View Code” file is **not** automatically fed into training.\n- Grok’s base model is trained on **massive public datasets** (web data, books, scientific papers, code repositories, etc.) in large offline training runs.\n- Individual user conversations are **not** used for daily training. xAI is very selective and cautious about using private user data.\n- Even when users opt-in to “Improve the model” or share data, conversations go through heavy filtering, anonymization, deduplication, and quality scoring. Only a tiny fraction of high-quality, consented conversations ever make it into training corpora — and even then, they are heavily transformed and mixed with other data.\n\nThe “View Code” button exists mainly so **you** can copy the full context, debug prompts, share it, or analyze how the model was instructed.\n\n### 2. Quick reference parts from it\n\nThe system **does** use structured conversation data in two practical ways:\n\n- **In-session memory / quick reference**: During a long conversation, Grok can create internal summaries or embeddings of key parts of the history. These act as “quick references” so the model doesn’t lose context even after many messages.\n- **Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)**: In some advanced modes or future memory features, the system can retrieve relevant snippets from past conversations you’ve had. The structured “View Code” format makes this retrieval cleaner and more accurate.\n\nSo while the full file itself is not directly used for training the base model, **structured conversation traces** like this are extremely valuable for improving long-context understanding, tool use, reasoning chains, and multi-turn coherence.\n\n### 3. How many versions of that type of document are created per day?\n\n**Realistic estimate: Very few (likely low hundreds to low thousands at most) actually reach training.**\n\n- Grok has millions of users and generates enormous numbers of conversations daily.\n- However, only a small percentage of users have data sharing enabled.\n- Of those, only conversations that meet strict quality, diversity, and safety filters are kept.\n- The “View Code” button itself is rarely clicked relative to total usage.\n\nxAI does **not** create one training file per conversation every day. Training runs are done periodically (weeks or months apart) on carefully curated datasets. Daily “versions” for training purposes are effectively close to zero for any single user conversation.\n\n---\n\n**Bottom line:**\n\nThe “View Code” feature is primarily for **you** — to see the full structured conversation, copy it, or understand how the prompt was built. It is not the main way the model learns. The model learns from vast public data and carefully filtered, aggregated, anonymized signals across millions of interactions.\n\nIf you want the model to improve from *this specific conversation*, the best things you can do are:\n- Use the “Improve the model” / data sharing toggle (if available).\n- Give explicit feedback using the thumbs up/down buttons.\n- Continue building high-quality, detailed, useful conversations like this one.\n\nWould you like me to show you what a cleaned “View Code” version of **this entire 42-message conversation** looks like in a training-friendly format? }\n    pub fn backspace(&mut self) { self.input_buffer.pop(); }\n    pub fn tick_metrics(&mut self) {\n        self.hot_metric = self.hot_metric.saturating_add(3);\n        self.cold_metric = self.cold_metric.saturating_add(1);\n    }\n    pub fn terminal_text(&self) -> String {\n        let mut s = self.output_lines.join(\"\\n\");\n        s.push_str(&format!(\"\\n> {}_\", self.input_buffer));\n        s\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**`ironraw/src/raw_kms.rs`** (from earlier)\n```rust\n// KMS dumb buffer implementation (as previously provided)\n```\n\n**`ironraw/assets/grok_font_indexes.json`** — use the JSON you had in earlier messages (or a minimal one with `mono_terminal` and `regular`).\n\n---\n\n### 3. Server (`server/Cargo.toml`)\n\n```toml\n[package]\nname = \"server\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2021\"\n\n[dependencies]\nironraw = { path = \"../ironraw\" }\naxum = { version = \"0.7\", features = [\"ws\"] }\ntokio = { version = \"1\", features = [\"full\"] }\nserde = { version = \"1.0\", features = [\"derive\"] }\nserde_json = \"1.0\"\nfutures-util = \"0.3\"\nuuid = { version = \"1.0\", features = [\"v4\", \"serde\"] }\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4. Server `server/src/main.rs` (Full, uses real `ironraw::IronSession`)\n\n```rust\nuse axum::{\n    extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade},\n    response::IntoResponse,\n    routing::get,\n    Router,\n};\nuse futures_util::{SinkExt, StreamExt};\nuse ironraw::IronSession;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\nuse tokio::sync::broadcast;\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\ntype SharedState = Arc>;\ntype Sessions = Arc>>;\ntype Tx = broadcast::Sender;\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct RailwayState {\n    active_agents: HashMap,\n    tasks: Vec,\n    is_paused: bool,\n    depth: u32,\n    last_intent: Option,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct Task {\n    id: String,\n    title: String,\n    stage: String,\n    status: String,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\")]\nenum ClientMessage {\n    Join { client: String },\n    ToggleAgent { name: String, value: bool },\n    CreateTask { title: String },\n    VoiceCommand { text: String },\n    Pause,\n    Resume,\n    ForceMerge,\n    TerminalInput { session_id: String, text: String },\n    TerminalBackspace { session_id: String },\n}\n\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() {\n    let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(RailwayState {\n        active_agents: HashMap::from([(\"Oppie\".into(), true), (\"Leo\".into(), true), (\"Enrico\".into(), true), (\"Hans\".into(), true)]),\n        tasks: vec![],\n        is_paused: false,\n        depth: 4,\n        last_intent: None,\n    }));\n\n    let sessions: Sessions = Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));\n    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::(512);\n\n    let app = Router::new().route(\"/ws\", get(move |ws: WebSocketUpgrade| async move {\n        ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state.clone(), sessions.clone(), tx.clone()))\n    }));\n\n    println!(\"🚄 Grok Railway v15 (Rust + Real IronRaw) running on ws://0.0.0.0:8080/ws\");\n    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\").await.unwrap();\n    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();\n}\n\nasync fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: SharedState, sessions: Sessions, tx: Tx) {\n    let client_id = format!(\"Client-{}\", Uuid::new_v4().simple());\n    let mut rx = tx.subscribe();\n\n    let session = IronSession::new(client_id.clone());\n    sessions.lock().unwrap().insert(client_id.clone(), session.clone());\n\n    // Send initial state\n    let current_state = state.lock().unwrap().clone();\n    let init = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"state\",\n        \"state\": current_state,\n        \"terminal_text\": session.terminal_text(),\n        \"client_id\": client_id\n    });\n    let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(init.to_string())).await;\n\n    loop {\n        tokio::select! {\n            Some(Ok(msg)) = socket.recv() => {\n                if let Message::Text(text) = msg {\n                    if let Ok(cmd) = serde_json::from_str::(&text) {\n                        handle_command(cmd, &state, &sessions, &tx, &client_id).await;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(update) = rx.recv() => {\n                let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(update)).await;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nasync fn handle_command(cmd: ClientMessage, state: &SharedState, sessions: &Sessions, tx: &Tx, client_id: &str) {\n    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();\n    let mut session_map = sessions.lock().unwrap();\n\n    match cmd {\n        ClientMessage::VoiceCommand { text } => {\n            let intent = classify_voice_command(&text); // You can add real logic here\n            s.last_intent = Some(intent.clone());\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[VOICE] {} → {}\", text, intent));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::TerminalInput { session_id, text } => {\n            if let Some(sess) = session_map.get_mut(&session_id) {\n                sess.input_buffer = text;\n                let resp = sess.submit_line();\n                log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[TERMINAL] {}: {}\", session_id, resp));\n            }\n        }\n        // ... (ToggleAgent, CreateTask, Pause, Resume, ForceMerge — same as previous version)\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    let update = serde_json::json!({ \"type\": \"state\", \"state\": s.clone() });\n    let _ = tx.send(update.to_string());\n}\n\nfn classify_voice_command(text: &str) -> String {\n    let lower = text.to_lowercase();\n    if lower.contains(\"pause\") { \"pause\".into() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"resume\") { \"resume\".into() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"merge\") { \"force_merge\".into() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"steno\") { \"export_steno\".into() }\n    else { \"unknown\".into() }\n}\n\nfn log_and_broadcast(tx: &Tx, message: &str) {\n    println!(\"{}\", message);\n    let log_msg = serde_json::json!({\"type\": \"log\", \"text\": message});\n    let _ = tx.send(log_msg.to_string());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### Next Steps for You in Cursor\n\n1. Full `src/main.rs`\n\n```rust\nuse axum::{\n    extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade},\n    response::IntoResponse,\n    routing::get,\n    Router,\n};\nuse futures_util::{SinkExt, StreamExt};\nuse ironraw::IronSession;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::path::Path;\nuse std::process::Command;\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\nuse tempfile::NamedTempFile;\nuse tokio::sync::broadcast;\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\ntype SharedState = Arc>;\ntype Sessions = Arc>>;\ntype Tx = broadcast::Sender;\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct RailwayState {\n    active_agents: HashMap,\n    tasks: Vec,\n    is_paused: bool,\n    depth: u32,\n    last_intent: Option,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]\nstruct Task {\n    id: String,\n    title: String,\n    stage: String,\n    status: String,\n}\n\n#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\")]\nenum ClientMessage {\n    Join { client: String },\n    ToggleAgent { name: String, value: bool },\n    CreateTask { title: String },\n    VoiceCommand { text: String },\n    AudioData { data: Vec },           // Raw audio blob from browser\n    Pause,\n    Resume,\n    ForceMerge,\n    TerminalInput { session_id: String, text: String },\n}\n\n// ====================== MAIN ======================\n#[tokio::main]\nasync fn main() {\n    let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(RailwayState {\n        active_agents: HashMap::from([\n            (\"Oppie\".into(), true),\n            (\"Leo\".into(), true),\n            (\"Enrico\".into(), true),\n            (\"Hans\".into(), true),\n        ]),\n        tasks: vec![],\n        is_paused: false,\n        depth: 4,\n        last_intent: None,\n    }));\n\n    let sessions: Sessions = Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));\n    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::(512);\n\n    let app = Router::new().route(\"/ws\", get(move |ws: WebSocketUpgrade| async move {\n        ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| {\n            handle_socket(socket, state.clone(), sessions.clone(), tx.clone())\n        })\n    }));\n\n    println!(\"\\n{}\", \"=\".repeat(80));\n    println!(\"🚄 GROK TEAM RAILWAY v15 — Rust Server with Whisper\");\n    println!(\"WebSocket: ws://0.0.0.0:8080/ws\");\n    println!(\"Hybrid STT: Browser first → Server-side Whisper fallback\");\n    println!(\"Make sure whisper.cpp binary is in PATH as 'whisper-cli'\");\n    println!(\"{}\", \"=\".repeat(80));\n\n    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(\"0.0.0.0:8080\").await.unwrap();\n    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();\n}\n\nasync fn handle_socket(\n    mut socket: WebSocket,\n    state: SharedState,\n    sessions: Sessions,\n    tx: Tx,\n) {\n    let client_id = format!(\"Client-{}\", Uuid::new_v4().simple());\n    let mut rx = tx.subscribe();\n\n    let session = IronSession::new(client_id.clone());\n    sessions.lock().unwrap().insert(client_id.clone(), session.clone());\n\n    println!(\"[Rust] {} connected — IronRaw session active\", client_id);\n\n    // Send initial state\n    let current = state.lock().unwrap().clone();\n    let init = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"state\",\n        \"state\": current,\n        \"terminal_text\": session.terminal_text(),\n        \"client_id\": client_id.clone()\n    });\n    let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(init.to_string())).await;\n\n    loop {\n        tokio::select! {\n            Some(Ok(msg)) = socket.recv() => {\n                match msg {\n                    Message::Binary(audio_data) => {\n                        log_and_broadcast(&tx, &format!(\"[AUDIO] Received {} bytes from {}\", audio_data.len(), client_id));\n                        let transcription = run_whisper_on_audio(&audio_data).await;\n                        log_and_broadcast(&tx, &format!(\"[WHISPER] {}\", transcription));\n                        \n                        if let Some(sess) = sessions.lock().unwrap().get_mut(&client_id) {\n                            sess.output_lines.push(format!(\"[STT] {}\", transcription));\n                        }\n                        \n                        let result = serde_json::json!({\n                            \"type\": \"stt_result\",\n                            \"text\": transcription,\n                            \"confidence\": 0.92\n                        });\n                        let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(result.to_string())).await;\n                    }\n                    Message::Text(text) => {\n                        if let Ok(cmd) = serde_json::from_str::(&text) {\n                            handle_command(cmd, &state, &sessions, &tx, &client_id).await;\n                        }\n                    }\n                    Message::Close(_) => break,\n                    _ => {}\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(update) = rx.recv() => {\n                let _ = socket.send(Message::Text(update)).await;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    sessions.lock().unwrap().remove(&client_id);\n    println!(\"[Rust] {} disconnected\", client_id);\n}\n\nasync fn handle_command(\n    cmd: ClientMessage,\n    state: &SharedState,\n    sessions: &Sessions,\n    tx: &Tx,\n    client_id: &str,\n) {\n    let mut s = state.lock().unwrap();\n    let mut session_map = sessions.lock().unwrap();\n\n    match cmd {\n        ClientMessage::VoiceCommand { text } => {\n            let intent = classify_voice_command(&text);\n            s.last_intent = Some(intent.clone());\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[VOICE] {} → {}\", text, intent));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::TerminalInput { session_id, text } => {\n            if let Some(sess) = session_map.get_mut(&session_id) {\n                sess.input_buffer = text;\n                let resp = sess.submit_line();\n                log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[TERMINAL] {}: {}\", session_id, resp));\n            }\n        }\n        ClientMessage::ToggleAgent { name, value } => {\n            s.active_agents.insert(name.clone(), value);\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[AGENT] {} toggled to {}\", name, value));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::CreateTask { title } => {\n            let task = Task {\n                id: format!(\"R{}\", 1000 + s.tasks.len()),\n                title,\n                stage: \"POLL\".to_string(),\n                status: \"ON RAIL\".to_string(),\n            };\n            s.tasks.insert(0, task);\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, &format!(\"[TASK] New task created\"));\n        }\n        ClientMessage::Pause => { s.is_paused = true; log_and_broadcast(tx, \"[SYSTEM] Railway paused\"); }\n        ClientMessage::Resume => { s.is_paused = false; log_and_broadcast(tx, \"[SYSTEM] Railway resumed\"); }\n        ClientMessage::ForceMerge => {\n            for t in &mut s.tasks { t.status = \"MERGED\".to_string(); }\n            log_and_broadcast(tx, \"[HITL] Force merge executed\");\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n\n    let update = serde_json::json!({ \"type\": \"state\", \"state\": s.clone() });\n    let _ = tx.send(update.to_string());\n}\n\nfn classify_voice_command(text: &str) -> String {\n    let lower = text.to_lowercase();\n    if lower.contains(\"pause\") { \"pause\".into() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"resume\") { \"resume\".into() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"merge\") { \"force_merge\".into() }\n    else if lower.contains(\"steno\") { \"export_steno\".into() }\n    else { \"unknown\".into() }\n}\n\nasync fn run_whisper_on_audio(audio_data: &[u8]) -> String {\n    // Save to temporary WAV file\n    let temp_file = match tempfile::Builder::new()\n        .prefix(\"whisper_audio_\")\n        .suffix(\".webm\")\n        .tempfile()\n    {\n        Ok(f) => f,\n        Err(_) => return \"Failed to create temp file\".to_string(),\n    };\n\n    if let Err(e) = std::fs::write(temp_file.path(), audio_data) {\n        return format!(\"Failed to write audio: {}\", e);\n    }\n\n    // Call whisper.cpp CLI (assumes `whisper-cli` is in PATH)\n    let output = Command::new(\"whisper-cli\")\n        .arg(temp_file.path())\n        .arg(\"--model\")\n        .arg(\"models/ggml-base.en.bin\")   // Change to your model\n        .arg(\"--output-txt\")\n        .output();\n\n    match output {\n        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => {\n            String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()\n        }\n        Ok(o) => format!(\"Whisper failed: {}\", String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr)),\n        Err(e) => format!(\"Failed to run whisper-cli: {}\", e),\n    }\n}\n\nfn log_and_broadcast(tx: &Tx, message: &str) {\n    println!(\"{}\", message);\n    let log_msg = serde_json::json!({\n        \"type\": \"log\",\n        \"text\": message\n    });\n    let _ = tx.send(log_msg.to_string());\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### How to Use\n\n1. Put the Rust code in `server/src/main.rs`\n2. Make sure you have **whisper.cpp** built and `whisper-cli` available in your PATH.\n3. Run the server with `cargo run`\n\nThe dashboard will now try the browser first, and if confidence is low or you say “use whisper”, it records raw audio and sends it to this Rust server, which runs Whisper and returns the result.\n\nWould you like me to also give you:\n- The matching updated dashboard with better audio handling?\n- A Dockerfile for this Rust + Whisper setup?\n- Instructions on how to build and use `whisper.cpp` with the best model for this use case?\n\nJust say the word."
